Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on clusters structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 560. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 426 with relations in degree 2.

This completes the proof. QED.
